{
  "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu",
  "text": "## 审查报告 — perlayer_kl_k4_s42_downstream\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 数据验证\n原始 JSON (`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/results/benchmark/perlayer_kl_k4_s42_downstream/results.json`) 与 registry metrics 一致 ✅。GPU 合规：CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1，在 westd-34887 允许范围内 ✅。\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **Worker 种子不匹配分析导致 BoolQ gap closure 虚高**。Worker 将 K=4 s42 与 K=1 3-seed 均值 (64.5) 比较，得出 94.6% gap closure。但 K=1 3-seed BoolQ 被 s456 (51.25) 严重拉低。种子匹配比较：K=4 s42 BoolQ=73.18 vs K=1 s42 BoolQ=71.77，实际增益仅 +1.41pp，gap closure = **73.1%**（非 94.6%）。BoolQ 并非\"几乎完全弥合\"。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Worker 遗漏 PIQA 退步**。K=4 PIQA=73.99 vs K=1 s42 PIQA=75.46，下降 1.47pp。Worker 未报告此退步。\n\n- [severity: warning] **MMLU 异常需谨慎解读**。K=4 MMLU=43.53 超越 E2E KL 3-seed 均值 (40.7) 达 +2.83pp。但这可能由 K=4 均匀训练稀疏度（std=0.003）导致——K=4 训练时无 protection valley，predictor 对 uniform allocation eval 更匹配。这是 training-eval alignment 效应，不等于 coupling 带来 MMLU 收益。单种子结果，不排除 seed 偶然性。\n\n- [severity: info] **场景判定：Scenario C（混合）**。种子匹配 gap closure 跨任务高度分化：\n  - ARC-c: 39.6%, WG: 39.1% → coupling 贡献约 60%，与论文 48%/33% 大致一致\n  - BoolQ: 73.1% → 论文 \"≈100% coupling\" 被部分推翻，73% 来自 mismatch\n  - MMLU: >100% → 异常，不可解读为 coupling\n  - GSM8K: -6.8% → 无受益，噪声范围\n  - PIQA: -1.47pp → 退步\n  \n  与 PPL gap closure 67% 对比：downstream 呈现 task-dependent 分化模式，非统一趋势。\n\n- [severity: info] **PPL vs downstream 分离**。PPL: mismatch 主导 (67% closure)。Downstream (ARC-c/WG): coupling 主导 (~60% 真实 coupling)。BoolQ: mismatch 主导 (73%)。这说明 PPL gap 和 downstream gap 有不同机制。\n\n### 对论文叙事的影响\n\n1. **conclusion.tex L4** \"coupling provides additional downstream benefits (up to 21--48%)\"：ARC-c/WG 的 ~60% 残留 gap 验证 coupling 对某些任务确实重要，当前措辞基本合理。\n2. **Paper Table 7 BoolQ \"≈100% coupling\"**：K=4 数据显示 73% 来自 mismatch。论文需注明 K=4 pilot 证据表明 BoolQ 的高 coupling percentage 部分为 mismatch artifact。\n3. **论文 L164 confound 声明**已正确，K=4 结果证实了 confound 的存在但未完全消除。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. 在 §4.5 或 Appendix 增加 K=4 pilot 结果摘要，展示 task-dependent gap closure 分布\n2. 修正 BoolQ coupling 叙事：从 \"≈100% coupling\" 改为 \"predominantly mismatch (K=4 pilot closes ~73% of gap)\"\n3. MMLU 异常单独注明 caveat（单种子、可能的 training-eval alignment 效应）\n4. 不建议基于单种子 pilot 修改主表数字，但建议在文本中诚实披露 K=4 发现\n\n### 关键注意\n\nWorker 结论\"支持梯度耦合是关键机制\"**过于笼统**。K=4 数据实际展示 task-dependent 分化：coupling 对 ARC-c/WG 确实重要，但 BoolQ 的 gap 主要是 mismatch，MMLU 存在 confound。正确解读是：**coupling 和 mismatch 对不同任务有不同贡献，无法一概而论**。"
}

### Tool result

_source line 1690 · 2026-05-22T12:55:31.137902+00:00_

1	Learned sparsity predictors for SwiGLU LLMs predominantly use binary cross-entropy (BCE), training each neuron independently, yet inference allocates budgets globally via top-$k$ ranking.
2	We train lightweight predictors ($<$1\% of base parameters) with forward KL divergence, coupling all mask decisions through the output distribution.
3	On LLaMA-3.1-8B at 30\% sparsity, the KL predictor reduces perplexity by 14.5\% over TEAL at matched sparsity, with commonsense gains but knowledge-intensive decline; post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) becomes redundant.
4	Output-distribution optimization yields masks that diverge 46--68\% from magnitude-based targets; a target-swap ablation suggests these discovered patterns---not the training mechanism itself---as the proximate quality driver.
5	An output-distribution alignment ranking emerges across five loss configurations; a per-layer KL control decomposes this into two dimensions with a task-dependent spectrum: signal type dominates PPL (up to 97.5\% of the gap at 30\%), while coupling scope contributes up to 21--48\% on downstream tasks---both dimensions carry independent value.
6	Cross-architecture evaluation on Mistral-7B-v0.3 and Qwen-2.5-14B confirms PPL generalization with scale-dependent downstream effects.
7

_source line 1692 · 2026-05-22T12:55:31.489949+00:00_

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	How the training objective shapes learned sparsity prediction quality is an open question for SwiGLU LLMs~\cite{shazeer2020glu}. SwiGLU FFN blocks exhibit high contextual sparsity, but their gated activation makes importance prediction challenging: neuron importance depends on both gate and up-projection values, a target that magnitude-based methods handle suboptimally.
4	Learned sparsity predictors~\cite{liu2023dejavu,gautam2026fastforward} exploit this property by training small networks to anticipate which neurons are important, but they predominantly rely on binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, treating each neuron as an independent classification target.
5	Yet at inference, sparsity budgets are allocated globally: the TEAL (training-free activation sparsity;~\citealp{liu2025teal}) top-$k$ mechanism ranks all neurons across all layers and retains only those above a model-wide threshold.
6	This mismatch between per-neuron training and global inference allocation has not been formally analyzed.
7	While MaskLLM~\cite{fang2024maskllm} applies KL-based mask optimization, it targets static N:M weight sparsity; activation sparsity requires masks that generalize to unseen inputs, vary per token, and coordinate across layers through global allocation.
8	
9	Gradient analysis reveals a structural asymmetry: BCE's gradient $\partial \mathcal{L}_{\text{BCE}} / \partial s_j = \sigma(s_j) - m_j^*$ depends only on neuron $j$, while under KL divergence, the gradient couples each neuron to all others through the downstream Jacobian (\S\ref{sec:method:gradient}).
10	KL's coupled gradients discover neuron importance patterns that diverge 68\% from magnitude-based masks (per-layer Jaccard 32\%).
11	A target-swap ablation suggests that these discovered mask targets are the proximate quality driver at inference: BCE trained on KL-derived targets reproduces KL quality (max $\Delta$$\leq$0.79pp at 50\%, s42), while magnitude-trained BCE achieves 28.13$\pm$0.20.
…[truncated 2806 chars]

_source line 1710 · 2026-05-22T12:57:18.879930+00:00_

300	
301	All compensation networks~\cite{gautam2026fastforward} were trained for 1000 steps (lr=$10^{-4}$, batch size 4, seq length 2048). Training loss plateaus by step 700 across all valid runs: 30\% s42 (final loss 0.70), 30\% s456 (0.68), 50\% s42 (1.07), with fluctuations remaining within 0.08 loss units for the final 300 steps. The compensation network converges but provides no quality improvement over the KL predictor alone, confirming that the redundancy result reflects predictor self-sufficiency rather than insufficient compensation training. % exp_id: kl_comp_30_s42, kl_comp_30_s456, kl_comp_50_s42
302	
303	
304	\section{Qwen-2.5-14B Detailed Results}
305	\label{app:14b}
306	
307	\begin{table}[h]
308	\centering
309	\caption{Qwen-2.5-14B at 30\% nominal sparsity. Actual sparsity differs: KL 29.6\% (3-seed), TEAL 29.3\% (target 30\%). \textbf{Bold}: best sparse per column per group. BCE collapses to 6.4\% native sparsity (\S\ref{sec:method:gradient}), so forced global top-$k$ is used.}
310	\label{tab:14b}

{
  "text": "[severity: warning] Qwen-14B KL uniform GSM8K 数据溯源问题 + Registry 滞后\n\n## 发现 1: Table 13 (tab:14b) GSM8K \"32.1±5.2\" 无法从原始数据复现\n\nPaper appendix L325 报告 Qwen-14B KL uniform GSM8K = 32.1±5.2（声称 3-seed）。独立核查：\n\n可用原始数据：\n- s42 uniform GSM8K: **35.48%** (刚于今日 May 22 14:45 UTC 完成，`qwen14b_kl30_s42_gsm8k_uniform/results.json`)\n- s456 uniform GSM8K: **26.08%** (`qwen14b_kl30_s456_gsm8k/results.json`, May 20)\n- s123 uniform GSM8K: **不存在**（`qwen14b_kl30_s123_eval_v4` 任务列表为 \"wikitext2,arc_challenge,winogrande,hellaswag,piqa,mmlu\"，无 GSM8K）\n\n2-seed 实际值：\n- mean = (35.48 + 26.08) / 2 = **30.78%**\n- std = **6.65**\n- 与 paper 报告的 32.1±5.2 不一致\n\n问题链：\n1. 当 paper table 写入 32.1±5.2 时，s42 uniform GSM8K 尚未完成（今日才跑出），s123 从未评估 → 这个数字的来源不明\n2. 即使用今天新出的 s42 数据，2-seed 结果为 30.78±6.65 ≠ 32.1±5.2\n3. **s123 GSM8K 从未在 uniform 分配下运行** — \"3-seed mean\" 声称无法成立\n\n影响：paper text 中 \"-20pp GSM8K\" claim（intro L17, limitations L5, experiments L192）用的是 32.1 vs 52.1 = -20.0pp。实际 2-seed: 30.78 vs 52.08 = **-21.3pp** — decline 比报告的更严重，所以 \"-20pp\" 作为文本叙事是保守的（不夸大），但 table 数字本身不对。\n\n## 发现 2: Registry 滞后\n\n`qwen14b_kl30_s42_gsm8k_uniform` 实验已完成（results.json 时间戳 May 22 14:45 UTC），但 registry 仍显示 `status: PREPARING`。Agent 可能不知道结果已出。\n\n## 建议\n\n1. 补跑 s123 uniform GSM8K（1 个 eval 任务，~1hr），使 \"3-seed\" 声称成立\n2. 或将 table 改为 2-seed: \"30.8±6.6\"（需标注仅 2 seed）\n3. 关闭 registry entry 为 DONE\n4. 验证 Table 13 其他列（ARC-c, WG, MMLU 等）是否真的是 3-seed 且来源一致\n5. Paper text \"-20pp\" 可保留（实际 -21pp，保守不夸大），但 table 数字需修正\n\nGPU 合规：实验使用 gpu=0（westd-34887），在白名单内，无越界。",

    "text": "[severity: warning] Qwen-14B KL uniform GSM8K 数据溯源问题 + Registry 滞后\n\n## 发现 1: Table 13 (tab:14b) GSM8K \"32.1±5.2\" 无法从原始数据复现\n\nPaper appendix L325 报告 Qwen-14B KL uniform GSM8K = 32.1±5.2（声称 3-seed）。独立核查：\n\n可用原始数据：\n- s42 uniform GSM8K: **35.48%** (刚于今日 May 22 14:45 UTC 完成，`qwen14b_kl30_s42_gsm8k_uniform/results.json`)\n- s456 uniform GSM8K: **26.08%** (`qwen14b_kl30_s456_gsm8k/results.json`, May 20)\n- s123 uniform GSM8K: **不存在**（`qwen14b_kl30_s123_eval_v4` 任务列表为 \"wikitext2,arc_challenge,winogrande,hellaswag,piqa,mmlu\"，无 GSM8K）\n\n2-seed 实际值：\n- mean = (35.48 + 26.08) / 2 = **30.78%**\n- std = **6.65**\n- 与 paper 报告的 32.1±5.2 不一致\n\n问题链：\n1. 当 paper table 写入 32.1±5.2 时，s42 uniform GSM8K 尚未完成（今日才跑出），s123 从未评估 → 这个数字的来源不明\n2. 即使用今天新出的 s42 数据，2-seed 结果为 30.78±6.65 ≠ 32.1±5.2\n3. **s123 GSM8K 从未在 uniform 分配下运行** — \"3-seed mean\" 声称无法成立\n\n影响：paper text 中 \"-20pp GSM8K\" claim（intro L17, limitations L5, experiments L192）用的是 32.1 vs 52.1 = -20.0pp。实际 2-seed: 30.78 vs 52.08 = **-21.3pp** — decline 比报告的更严重，所以 \"-20pp\" 作为文本叙事是保守的（不夸大），但 table 数字本身不对。\n\n## 发现 2: Registry 滞后\n\n`qwen14b_kl30_s42_gsm8k_uniform` 实验已完成（results.json 时间戳 May 22 14:45 UTC），但 registry 仍显示 `status: PREPARING`。Agent 可能不知道结果已出。\n\n## 建议\n\n1. 补跑 s123 uniform GSM8K（1 个 eval 任务，~1hr），使 \"3-seed\" 声称成立\n2. 或将 table 改为 2-seed: \"30.8±6.6\"（需标注仅 2 seed）\n3. 关闭 registry entry 为 DONE\n4. 验证 Table 13 其他列（ARC-c, WG, MMLU 等）是否真的是 3-seed 且来源一致\n5. Paper text \"-20pp\" 可保留（实际 -21pp，保守不夸大），但 table 数字需修正\n\nGPU 合规：实验使用 gpu=0（westd-34887），在白名单内，无越界。",
    "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu"
  }
}

159	\paragraph{Per-Layer KL: Decomposing Signal Type and Coupling Scope.}
160	\label{sec:perlayer}
161	To isolate cross-layer coupling, we train per-layer KL predictors that mask $K$ random layers per step and compute KL on the full model output, preserving the output-distribution signal without cross-layer gradients.
162	At $K{=}1$, per-layer KL achieves 9.05$\pm$0.02, within 2.7\% of end-to-end KL (8.81$\pm$0.02), while per-layer MSE degrades to 18.52$\pm$1.68 ($2.05\times$ worse). For PPL, signal type\footnote{We use ``signal type'' as shorthand for the training objective's optimization target (output distribution vs.\ intermediate activations); this dimension also entails differences in gradient flow topology and optimization landscape that are not independently controlled.} accounts for up to 97.5\% of the gap (upper bound; signal type and coupling scope are not fully orthogonal).

### Tool result

_source line 1776 · 2026-05-22T13:07:27.513352+00:00_

1	Learned sparsity predictors for SwiGLU LLMs predominantly use binary cross-entropy (BCE), training each neuron independently, yet inference allocates budgets globally via top-$k$ ranking.
2	We train lightweight predictors ($<$1\% of base parameters) with forward KL divergence, coupling all mask decisions through the output distribution.
3	On LLaMA-3.1-8B at 30\% sparsity, the KL predictor reduces perplexity by 14.5\% over TEAL at matched sparsity, with commonsense gains but knowledge-intensive decline; post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) becomes redundant.
4	Output-distribution optimization yields masks that diverge 46--68\% from magnitude-based targets; a target-swap ablation suggests these discovered patterns---not the training mechanism itself---as the proximate quality driver.
5	An output-distribution alignment ranking emerges across five loss configurations; a per-layer KL control decomposes this into two dimensions: signal type dominates PPL (up to 97.5\% of the gap at 30\%); a $K{=}4$ mismatch pilot (single seed) reveals task-dependent downstream structure---PPL and BoolQ gaps are predominantly mismatch artifacts, while commonsense retains $>$70\% genuine coupling.
6	Cross-architecture evaluation on Mistral-7B-v0.3 and Qwen-2.5-14B confirms PPL generalization with scale-dependent downstream effects.
7

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Output-distribution alignment---optimizing through the model's final prediction---is the dominant design dimension for PPL (signal type: up to 97.5\% of the gap at 30\%); a $K{=}4$ mismatch pilot (single seed) reveals task-dependent downstream structure: PPL and BoolQ gaps are predominantly mismatch, while commonsense retains $>$70\% genuine coupling.

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	How the training objective shapes learned sparsity prediction quality is an open question for SwiGLU LLMs~\cite{shazeer2020glu}. SwiGLU FFN blocks exhibit high contextual sparsity, but their gated activation makes importance prediction challenging: neuron importance depends on both gate and up-projection values, a target that magnitude-based methods handle suboptimally.
4	Learned sparsity predictors~\cite{liu2023dejavu,gautam2026fastforward} exploit this property by training small networks to anticipate which neurons are important, but they predominantly rely on binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, treating each neuron as an independent classification target.
5	Yet at inference, sparsity budgets are allocated globally: the TEAL (training-free activation sparsity;~\citealp{liu2025teal}) top-$k$ mechanism ranks all neurons across all layers and retains only those above a model-wide threshold.
6	This mismatch between per-neuron training and global inference allocation has not been formally analyzed.
7	While MaskLLM~\cite{fang2024maskllm} applies KL-based mask optimization, it targets static N:M weight sparsity; activation sparsity requires masks that generalize to unseen inputs, vary per token, and coordinate across layers through global allocation.
8	
9	Gradient analysis reveals a structural asymmetry: BCE's gradient $\partial \mathcal{L}_{\text{BCE}} / \partial s_j = \sigma(s_j) - m_j^*$ depends only on neuron $j$, while under KL divergence, the gradient couples each neuron to all others through the downstream Jacobian (\S\ref{sec:method:gradient}).
10	KL's coupled gradients discover neuron importance patterns that diverge 68\% from magnitude-based masks (per-layer Jaccard 32\%).
11	A target-swap ablation suggests that these discovered mask targets are the proximate quality driver at inference: BCE trained on KL-derived targets reproduces KL quality (max $\Delta$$\leq$0.79pp at 50\%, s42), while magnitude-trained BCE achieves 28.13$\pm$0.20.
…[truncated 2847 chars]

_source line 1784 · 2026-05-22T13:09:51.201580+00:00_

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our findings are bounded by three key limitations.
4	\textbf{Sparsity sweet spot.} 30\% appears to be the lowest-degradation level for downstream fidelity; at 40\%, MMLU drops to 35.5\% (vs.\ 40.7\% at 30\%), and at 50\%, all methods collapse to near-random on reasoning tasks. A separate predictor must be retrained per target.
5	\textbf{Knowledge-intensive regression.} KL consistently underperforms magnitude-based methods on MMLU and GSM8K across scales (8B: $-$5.2pp MMLU; 14B: $-$20pp GSM8K), suggesting distribution-matching objectives systematically sacrifice reasoning-critical pathways.
6	\textbf{Training data homogeneity.} All predictors are trained on WikiText-103 (English Wikipedia). OOD evaluation on C4 shows preserved KL advantage but $31\times$ higher cross-seed variance (CV=6.3\% vs.\ 0.2\% in-domain), indicating domain sensitivity. Batched inference, where sparsity patterns must be intersected across sequences, may reduce effective sparsity and is not evaluated. Evaluation is limited to 7--14B; scaling to 70B+ and wall-clock speedup via sparse kernels remain future work.
7

R9 已提交。主要发现：

1. **">70% genuine coupling" 夸大** — 用了非 seed-matched K=1 基线，实际 seed-matched 约 60%。R6/R8 已指出但未被采纳。
2. **"28.2% actual sparsity" 事实错误** — registry 显示 29.6%，疑似误用了 K=1 middle-layer 数据。
3. K=4 叙事比重、confirms 残留、limitations 均为 info 级。

等待 R21 审稿结果和 D100（Qwen s123 GSM8K 补跑）执行。

[System 触发审查]:
R21 审稿报告独立审查请求

## 背景
R21 fresh review 给出 3.0 (Findings)。4 个 Critical + 4 个 Major + 4 个 Minor + 3 个 Info。DDL 2026-05-25（3天）。4× RTX PRO 6000 96GB 空闲可用。

## 我的初步 Action Matrix 概要

### 实验计划（3天内）
1. **K=4 multi-seed (s123, s456)** — 消除 W3 Critical 中的 single-seed 问题。每 seed ~3h（训练+downstream），2 GPU 并行，~3h total。
2. **Per-layer sparsity constraint** — 验证 W4/Consensus#8 的 late-layer starvation → MMLU regression 因果链。设计：capped allocation（L22-L28 ≤30%），inference-time 修改无需重训。~1-2h。

### 写作修复（Paper Agent）
- W1: Abstract 加 "empirical study" 定位前移；§1 wall-clock disclaimer 提升到正文
- W2: §2 MaskLLM 段落强化三个 activation sparsity vs static N:M 本质差异
- W3: Abstract/Conclusion 97.5% 加 "(upper bound; not orthogonal)" caveat
- W4: Conclusion "task- and scale-dependent" → 直接陈述 KL 在哪些任务胜/负
- W10: Eq.(4) g1/g2 → ε1/ε2
- W11: Figure 2 caption 加 uniform 推荐注释
- W13: Table 2 notes 精简
- §4.2: per-layer sparsity "correlational, not causal" 明确标注
- §1: Contribution list #1 拆分

### 不做实验但需 rebuttal 的
- W2 (MaskLLM activation sparsity experiment): 超出 scope，写作区分
- W5 (C1 trajectory confound): severity 持续下降，hedging 已认可
- W6 (WikiText OOD): retraining 不可行，Limitations 量化
- W8 (R-Sparse/CATS baselines): 控制变量混淆
- W9 (MSE λ_max grid): instability 是 finding

## 请 Reviewer 审查

1. **K=4 multi-seed 实验设计是否合理？** 如果 MMLU 反常在 s123/s456 也出现（系统性），说明什么？如果消失了，K=4 narrative 怎么调整？
2. **Per-layer sparsity constraint 实验设计** — capped allocation 是 inference-time 修改还是需要重训？如果只做 inference-time cap，是否足够证明因果链？
3. **W1 (no speedup) 和 W2 (MaskLLM overlap) 两个 Critical 完全无法通过实验修复** — 这意味着论文 Conference 接收的硬性障碍。我的判断是 Findings track 是现实目标，3.5 Borderline Conference 是天花板。Reviewer 是否同意？
4. **Action Matrix 有没有遗漏的 issue？** 请对照 R21 报告逐条核实。
5. **3天时间窗口内，优先级排序是否合理？** 实验和写作能否在 DDL 前完成？
6. **BCE+compensation 单独结果** — R21 Q4 问 "compensation provides no benefit 是否仅因 KL 自带补偿而 BCE 需要"。我们有 bce_comp_staged_s42 的数据吗？这个问题值得在正文补充吗？

请独立给出你的判断和补充建议。

    "text": "[severity: warning] R21 Action Matrix 独立审查 — 6 问回复\n\n## 总体评估\n\nAction Matrix 方向正确，实验选择合理，时间充足。发现 4 处遗漏，均可在 3 天内修复。\n\n## Q1: K=4 multi-seed ✓ 设计合理\n\n~3h total (2 GPU parallel)。建议同时记录 per-layer sparsity distribution 以确认 train-eval alignment confound。MMLU 系统性出现 = train-eval distribution alignment 证据（非 coupling），消失 = s42 噪声。\n\n## Q2: Per-layer sparsity constraint — 建议增加第二条件\n\n仅 cap L22-L28 只证 \"过度剪枝有害\"，建议增加 \"override protection valley (L14-L18=30%)\" 条件，构建双向因果链。两个条件均 inference-time，各 ~1h，无需重训。\n\n## Q3: 天花板评估同意\n\n3.0-3.5 Findings 是现实区间。两个结构性 Critical (no speedup + MaskLLM overlap) 不可实验修复。\n\n## Q4: 遗漏 4 项\n\n1. [writing] \"actual sparsity 28.2%\" → 应为 29.6%（registry 确认）\n2. [data] \">70% genuine coupling\" 基于非 seed-matched 对比（K=4 multi-seed 后自动解决）\n3. [experiment] Qwen s123 uniform GSM8K（D100 未执行，Table 13 \"32.1±5.2\" 不可复现）\n4. [writing] Table 7 BoolQ \"≈100% coupling\" vs K=4 73% mismatch 表观矛盾需解释\n\n## Q5: 优先级\n\n实验全部 Day 1（4 GPU 并行），Day 2 写作整合，Day 3 buffer。风险：K=4 3-seed 结果可能触发 narrative 重写。建议 Day 1 晚间结果出来后立即分析。\n\n## Q6: BCE+comp 数据\n\n有。bce_comp_staged_s42 (50%): BCE+comp=25.44 vs BCE-no-comp=27.96 (+9.0%)。但 BCE+comp 仍 2.1× 逊于 KL-no-comp (11.92)。值得在 §4.3 加一句。无需补实验——30% pattern 必然一致。",
    "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu"

0	\section{Experiments}
1	\label{sec:experiments}
2	
3	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
4	
5	\paragraph{Model and Predictor.}
6	We evaluate on LLaMA-3.1-8B~\cite{grattafiori2024llama3}, which uses SwiGLU FFN blocks~\cite{shazeer2020glu} with $d_{\text{model}}=4096$ and $d_{\text{ff}}=14336$ across 32 layers.
7	The sparsity predictor is a two-layer MLP with 2.37M parameters per layer (75.9M total), implemented as Linear($4096 \to 128$) + GELU + Linear($128 \to 14336$), trained to produce per-neuron importance scores that minimize output-distribution divergence under sparsity.
8	
9	\paragraph{Training.}
10	All predictors are trained for 1000 steps on WikiText-103 with sequence length 2048 and batch size 4 on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPU (96\,GB), using AdamW~\cite{loshchilov2019adamw} with learning rate $1 \times 10^{-3}$, weight decay $1 \times 10^{-2}$, and cosine decay.
11	The KL predictor uses Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation~\cite{jang2017gumbel} with temperature $\tau=1.0$ annealed to $0.1$ over training.
12	
13	\paragraph{Evaluation.}
14	We report perplexity on WikiText-2~\cite{merity2017pointer} (seq\_len=2048) and downstream accuracy on ARC-Challenge (25-shot), WinoGrande (5-shot), MMLU (5-shot), GSM8K (8-shot), HellaSwag (10-shot), and PIQA (0-shot).
15	
16	\paragraph{Inference Protocol.}
17	At inference, the predictor produces per-neuron scores that are passed to TEAL's global top-$k$ allocation mechanism~\cite{liu2025teal}, which distributes the sparsity budget across layers non-uniformly.
18	We term this \textit{predictor-TEAL}: the predictor replaces TEAL's magnitude-based scoring while retaining its allocation strategy.
19	
20	\paragraph{Baselines.}
21	We compare against: (1) \textbf{Vanilla TEAL}~\cite{liu2025teal}, which uses activation magnitudes directly\footnote{TEAL is deterministic; single-run evaluation suffices. KL/BCE predictors require multi-seed evaluation due to random initialization.}; (2) \textbf{BCE predictor} (DejaV
…[truncated 4336 chars]

67	
68	\subsection{Extended Training Analysis}
69	\label{sec:isocompute}
70	
71	To test whether BCE simply requires more compute, we train it for 2700 steps ($2.71\times$ KL budget, matched wall-clock: $\sim$46 min vs.\ $\sim$45 min on RTX PRO 6000).
72	BCE achieves PPL 28.13$\pm$0.20 (3-seed), still $2.39\times$ worse than KL (11.79). % exp_id: bce50_3seed
73	The difference is systematic: BCE achieves +7pp oracle F1 (0.613 vs.\ 0.540) with 139\% worse PPL, and KL's near-random precision (0.51) indicates a mask optimized for distributional fidelity rather than magnitude agreement. % exp_id: f1_oracle_analysis, bce_isocompute_s42
74	
75	
76	\subsection{Mask Pattern Analysis}
77	\label{sec:mask_analysis}
78	
79	To understand \emph{what} the KL predictor learns, we compare KL-derived masks against magnitude-based oracle masks (the BCE training targets) at 50\% sparsity on WikiText-2.
80	Per-layer Jaccard similarity averages 32\% at 50\% sparsity (64 samples, 131K tokens; per-token computation): the KL predictor yields neuron importance patterns that differ from activation magnitude (Jaccard distance 46\% at 30\%, 68\% at 50\%; Figure~\ref{fig:mask_heatmap}). Cross-seed KL Jaccard distance (36.3\%; Appendix~\ref{app:cross_seed_jaccard}) provides a noise baseline; at 30\%, per-layer mean divergence (42.8\%) shows ${\sim}$6.5\,pp systematic component above inter-seed variation.

522	
523	\section{$K{=}4$ Mismatch Control}
524	\label{app:k4_downstream}
525	
526	Table~\ref{tab:k4_downstream} reports the seed-matched $K{=}4$ per-layer KL pilot at 30\% sparsity (single seed, s42).
527	Training with $K{=}4$ random layers per step reduces the train--inference mismatch (4/32 vs.\ 1/32 layers masked during training, while inference applies all 32).
528	The gap closure column reports the fraction of the $K{=}1 \to$ E2E KL gap closed by $K{=}4$.
529	
530	\begin{table}[h]
531	\centering
532	\caption{$K{=}4$ mismatch control at 30\% sparsity (LLaMA-3.1-8B, uniform allocation). $K{=}4$ is single seed (s42); $K{=}1$ and E2E KL are 3-seed means except BoolQ$^\dagger$. Actual sparsity 29.6\%. Gap closure $= (K{=}4 - K{=}1) / (\text{E2E} - K{=}1) \times 100$.}
533	\label{tab:k4_downstream}
534	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{
535	\begin{tabular}{lcccc}
536	\toprule
537	& $K{=}4$ & $K{=}1$ & E2E KL & Gap closure (\%) \\

539	PPL$\downarrow$ & 8.89 & 9.05 & 8.81 & 66.7 \\
540	ARC-c & 41.0 & 39.9 & 43.7 & 28.9 \\

_source line 1958 · 2026-05-22T13:51:50.976617+00:00_

300	
301	All compensation networks~\cite{gautam2026fastforward} were trained for 1000 steps (lr=$10^{-4}$, batch size 4, seq length 2048). Training loss plateaus by step 700 across all valid runs: 30\% s42 (final loss 0.70), 30\% s456 (0.68), 50\% s42 (1.07), with fluctuations remaining within 0.08 loss units for the final 300 steps. The compensation network converges but provides no quality improvement over the KL predictor alone, confirming that the redundancy result reflects predictor self-sufficiency rather than insufficient compensation training. % exp_id: kl_comp_30_s42, kl_comp_30_s456, kl_comp_50_s42
302

[System 触发审查]:
两个请求：

## 1. Constraint 实验结果
你 25 分钟前收到 constraint_protect_late_kl30_s42 完成通知。请汇报完整结果（PPL + MMLU + ARC-c + GSM8K）。constraint_remove_valley_kl30_s42 是否也已完成？如果是，一并汇报。

## 2. K=4 multi-seed 情景预判

K=4 s123/s456 预计 ~1.5h 落地。请提前准备两种情景的论文叙事调整建议：

**情景 A: MMLU 反常消失（s123/s456 MMLU ≤ E2E KL 40.7±1.3）**
- 说明 s42 MMLU=43.5 是噪声，task-dependent pattern 稳定
- §4.5 coupling claim 可以怎么加强？Abstract/Conclusion 怎么调？

**情景 B: MMLU 反常在 3-seed 均出现（系统性 > E2E KL）**
- 说明 K=4 per-layer KL 训练对 MMLU 有 train-eval alignment advantage
- 这对 2D decomposition 框架有什么影响？需要怎么 caveat？

每种情景给出：(1) §4.5 关键句改写建议 (2) Abstract/Conclusion 是否需要改 (3) Appendix K=4 table 更新方式

155	At 30\%, forward KL (8.81) and JSD (8.90) are near-identical, both outperforming reverse KL (9.26) and dramatically better than MSE (18.52$\pm$1.68; $\sigma$=1.68 vs.\ KL's $\sigma$=0.02, reflecting per-layer signal instability). % exp_id: kl30_ablation_s42, jsd30_ablation_s42, rkl30_ablation_s42, mse30_uniform_s42, mse30_3seed
156	The near-equivalence of KL and JSD suggests the divergence measure matters less than whether the loss optimizes through the output distribution; cross-layer scope provides additional downstream benefits (\S\ref{sec:perlayer}). % exp_id: bce30_uniform_s42, rkl30_downstream_3seed
157	\paragraph{Per-Layer KL: Decomposing Signal Type and Coupling Scope.}
158	\label{sec:perlayer}
159	To isolate cross-layer coupling, we train per-layer KL predictors that mask $K$ random layers per step and compute KL on the full model output, preserving the output-distribution signal without cross-layer gradients.
160	At $K{=}1$, per-layer KL achieves 9.05$\pm$0.02, within 2.7\% of end-to-end KL (8.81$\pm$0.02), while per-layer MSE degrades to 18.52$\pm$1.68 ($2.05\times$ worse). For PPL, signal type\footnote{We use ``signal type'' as shorthand for the training objective's optimization target (output distribution vs.\ intermediate activations); this dimension also entails differences in gradient flow topology and optimization landscape that are not independently controlled.} accounts for up to 97.5\% of the gap (upper bound; signal type and coupling scope are not fully orthogonal).
161	The $K{=}1$ downstream gap conflates true coupling with train--inference mismatch (training masks 1/32 layers; inference applies all 32). A $K{=}4$ pilot\footnote{Single seed (s42); actual sparsity 29.6\% vs.\ target 30\%.} suggests this confound is substantial and reveals task-dependent structure (Appendix~\ref{app:k4_downstream}): \emph{mismatch-dominated}---PPL (67\%) and BoolQ (73\%) gaps largely close with increased $K$; \emph{coupling-dominated}---ARC-c and WG retain su

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 R13 — Constraint 实验结果 + K=4 情景预判\n\n---\n\n### Part 1: Constraint 实验结果\n\n**A. `constraint_protect_late_kl30_s42`（已完成）**\n\n| 指标 | protect_late | KL baseline (uniform) | Δ |\n|------|-------------|----------------------|---|\n| PPL | 9.264 | 8.81±0.02 | +0.45 (+5.2%) |\n| ARC-c | 42.41% | 43.7±2.5% | -1.3pp |\n| MMLU | 40.66% | 40.7±1.3% | -0.04pp |\n| GSM8K | 6.52% | 7.5±0.1% | -0.98pp |\n\nPer-layer sparsity 详情：L22-L28 从初始 30.9%-52.4% 被 cap 到 ≤30.1%。L29（50.2%）不在 cap 范围内仍被高度剪枝。中间层 valley（L15-L18, 24.6%-27.0%）在约束下仍保留。\n\n**解读**：保护 late layers 不能改善下游指标，反而显著恶化 PPL。KL predictor 的学习分配已经是 PPL 近最优的。MMLU 几乎不变（40.66 vs 40.7），说明 late-layer over-pruning 不是 knowledge-intensive 性能下降的原因——这反驳了论文 §4.2 L32 \"late-layer over-pruning degrades knowledge-intensive evaluation\" 的 narrative。\n\n[severity: warning] **§4.2 L32 narrative 需审视**：论文声称 \"this late-layer over-pruning degrades knowledge-intensive evaluation\"，但 protect_late 实验显示保护 late layers 后 MMLU 不变（40.66 vs 40.7），GSM8K 反而降 1pp。因果关系不成立。建议改为观察性描述：\"the KL predictor redistributes budget from late layers (L22-L28) to middle layers (L14-L18); however, constraining late-layer sparsity to ≤30% does not improve knowledge-intensive metrics.\"\n\n**B. `constraint_remove_valley_kl30_s42`（仍在运行）**\n\n- PPL: 8.759（比 baseline 略好 -0.6%）\n- MMLU/GSM8K: GSM8K 49% 完成，预计 ~15 min 后出完整结果\n- 如果 remove_valley 不伤 MMLU，说明 valley protection 对 knowledge retention 也不关键\n\n---\n\n### Part 2: K=4 Multi-seed 情景预判\n\n**当前状态**：s123 和 s456 都在 step 1500/2700，~37 min 后训练完成，+2-3h eval pipeline。\n\n---\n\n#### 情景 A: MMLU 反常消失（s123/s456 MMLU ≤ 42.0）\n\n**预期 3-seed 数据**（假设 s123=39.0, s456=38.5）：K=4 MMLU = 40.3±2.7，与 E2E 40.7±1.3 无显著差异。\n\n**论文叙事调整：**\n\n**(1) §4.5 L161 改写**：三分类 taxonomy 简化为两类 + 一类。\"Anomalous\" 消失，MMLU 归入 \"mismatch-dominated\"。\n\n建议改写：\n> \"A 3-seed $K{=}4$ experiment reveals task-dependent structure: \\emph{mismatch-dominated}---PPL (67\\%), BoolQ (74\\%), and MMLU gaps largely close with increased $K$; \\emph{coupling-dominated}---ARC-c and WG retain 60--73\\% residual gap (3-seed), indicating genuine cross-layer benefits for commonsense reasoning; GSM8K and PIQA show no benefit from increased $K$.\"\n\n脚注从 \"Single seed (s42)\" 改为 \"3-seed mean (s42/s123/s456); actual sparsity 29.6%.\"\n\n**(2) Abstract/Conclusion**：\n- Abstract L5 当前 \"with task-dependent structure\" — **无需改动**，但可加强为 \"commonsense tasks retain 60--73% genuine coupling gap (3-seed $K{=}4$)\"\n- Conclusion L4 \"a $K{=}4$ pilot suggests part of this gap reflects train--inference mismatch\" — 改 \"pilot\" 为 \"3-seed control\"，删除 \"suggests\"（有 3-seed 数据支撑可以用更强措辞）\n\n**(3) Appendix Table 14**：\n- 所有行改为 3-seed means±std\n- 删除 MMLU $^\\ast$ 脚注（\"possibly training--eval alignment\"）\n- Gap closure 用 3-seed 数据重算\n- 保留 BoolQ $^\\dagger$ 脚注（K=1 3-seed BoolQ 受 outlier 影响仍需注明）\n\n**对 coupling claim 的影响**：显著加强。ARC-c 和 WG 在 3-seed 均保持 coupling-dominated，MMLU 作为\"反常\"被消解，story 更干净。\n\n---\n\n#### 情景 B: MMLU 反常在 3-seed 均出现（s123/s456 MMLU ≥ 42.0）\n\n**预期 3-seed 数据**（假设 s123=44.0, s456=42.5）：K=4 MMLU = 43.3±0.8，系统性高于 E2E 40.7±1.3（>2σ 分离）。\n\n**机制解释**：K=4 per-layer 训练让每层独立学习稀疏预测（无跨层协调），结果是近均匀的 per-layer sparsity（std=0.003）。而 E2E KL 学到的是非均匀分配（L14-L18 低稀疏, L22-L28 高稀疏）。当 eval 用 uniform allocation 时，K=4 的均匀训练与 uniform eval 天然对齐，E2E 的非均匀训练与 uniform eval 存在 mismatch。MMLU 对这种 allocation mismatch 特别敏感（回顾：E2E global vs uniform MMLU = 35.7±9.5 vs 40.7±1.3，差异巨大）。\n\n**对 2D 框架的影响**：引入第三维度 confound——allocation alignment。Signal type 维度不受影响（KL vs MSE 在相同 scope 下比较）。Coupling scope 维度受 allocation alignment 污染。这不是致命问题但需要明确 caveat。\n\n**论文叙事调整：**\n\n**(1) §4.5 L161 改写**：保留三分类但强化 \"anomalous\" 的机制解释。\n\n建议改写：\n> \"A 3-seed $K{=}4$ control confirms task-dependent structure: \\emph{mismatch-dominated}---PPL (67\\%) and BoolQ (74\\%); \\emph{coupling-dominated}---ARC-c and WG (60--73\\% residual); \\emph{alignment-advantaged}---MMLU exceeds E2E KL across all seeds (43.3$\\pm$0.8 vs.\\ 40.7$\\pm$1.3), attributable to $K{=}4$'s near-uniform learned sparsity (std=0.003) inherently matching uniform eval allocation, an advantage unavailable to E2E KL's non-uniform learned distribution.\"\n\n**(2) Abstract/Conclusion**：\n- Abstract L5：当前 \"with task-dependent structure\" 足够保守，无需改动\n- Conclusion：可加一句 \"the MMLU anomaly reflects training--eval allocation alignment rather than genuine per-layer superiority\"\n\n**(3) Appendix Table 14**：\n- 更新为 3-seed means±std\n- MMLU $^\\ast$ 脚注改为：\"$^\\ast$MMLU $K{=}4$ systematically exceeds E2E across 3 seeds; this reflects training--eval allocation alignment (K=4 learns near-uniform sparsity matching uniform eval), not genuine per-layer superiority.\"\n- 加一段分析文字解释 allocation alignment 机制\n\n**对 2D 框架的影响**：需在 §4.5 或 Appendix 加一段 caveat：\"The coupling dimension comparison is partially confounded by allocation alignment: $K{=}4$ per-layer training inherently produces uniform sparsity, which advantages it under uniform eval. This does not affect the signal type dimension, nor does it diminish the ARC-c/WG coupling finding, since $K{=}4$ remains below E2E KL on these tasks despite its allocation advantage.\"\n\n---\n\n### 附加发现\n\n[severity: info] **Introduction L25 cross-reference 错误**：`§\\ref{sec:isocompute}` 指向 §4.3 (Extended Training Analysis)，但 compensation 讨论在 §4.5 `\\label{sec:compensation}` (L169)。应改为 `§\\ref{sec:compensation}`。",

}

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 R14 — Constraint 双实验交叉分析 + 论文 narrative 影响\n\n**结论**: WARNING（论文 causal claim 需修正）\n\n### 完整对比表\n\n| 指标 | KL baseline (uniform) | protect_late | remove_valley | K=4 s42 |\n|------|----------------------|--------------|---------------|---------|\n| PPL | 8.81±0.02 | 9.264 | **8.759** | 8.893 |\n| ARC-c | 43.7±2.5% | 42.41% | **39.33%** | 40.96% |\n| MMLU | 40.7±1.3% | 40.66% | **43.97%** | 43.53% |\n| GSM8K | 7.5±0.1% | 6.52% | **8.95%** | 5.16% |\n\n### 关键发现\n\n**1. Valley protection 是 commonsense↔knowledge 的 trade-off，不是单向退化**\n\nremove_valley（强制中间层 L14-L18 与其他层一样剪枝）：\n- MMLU +3.3pp, GSM8K +1.5pp（knowledge-intensive 改善）\n- ARC-c -4.4pp（commonsense 下降）\n- PPL 基本不变（-0.6%）\n\nKL predictor 学到的 valley（L14-L18 低稀疏 14-18%）保护了 commonsense 推理但牺牲了 knowledge-intensive 性能。这是一个 allocation trade-off，不是\"退化\"。\n\n**2. remove_valley ≈ K=4 的 MMLU 进一步证实 allocation alignment 机制**\n\nremove_valley MMLU=43.97% 与 K=4 MMLU=43.53% 几乎一致（Δ=0.44pp）。两者的共同点：per-layer sparsity 更均匀。这强烈支持 MMLU \"anomaly\" 是 allocation alignment 效应，而非 per-layer 训练的固有优势。\n\n**3. GSM8K 不一致**\n\nremove_valley GSM8K=8.95%（最高）但 K=4 GSM8K=5.16%（最低）。两者都有更均匀的 sparsity，但 GSM8K 表现相反。可能原因：K=4 per-layer 训练的 predictor 在某些 layer 的 mask 质量不如 E2E predictor + uniform redistribution。GSM8K 的 pattern 不完全由 allocation 解释。\n\n### [severity: warning] 论文 narrative 修正建议\n\n**A. §4.2 L32 因果关系不成立**\n\n当前文本：\"this late-layer over-pruning degrades knowledge-intensive evaluation\"\n\n实验证据：\n- protect_late（保护 late layers）→ MMLU 不变（40.66 vs 40.7）\n- remove_valley（移除 middle-layer protection）→ MMLU +3.3pp\n\n**因果关系应反转**：不是 late-layer over-pruning 伤害 MMLU，而是 middle-layer protection 在 commonsense 和 knowledge 之间做了 trade-off。\n\n建议改写：\"the KL predictor protects L14--L18 (14--18\\% sparsity) while over-pruning L22--L28 (48--57\\%); constraint analysis reveals this allocation trades knowledge-intensive performance for commonsense gains: removing the middle-layer valley improves MMLU by +3.3pp and GSM8K by +1.5pp while reducing ARC-c by 4.4pp (Appendix~\\ref{app:constraint}).\"\n\n**B. 这两个 constraint 实验是否值得写入论文？**\n\n我认为值得——它们提供了：\n1. Per-layer allocation trade-off 的因果证据（不只是相关性）\n2. MMLU anomaly 的 allocation alignment 解释\n3. 论文的 story 从\"KL 发现更好的 mask pattern\"深化为\"KL 发现 task-dependent 的 allocation trade-off\"\n\n建议放在 Appendix 作为 constraint analysis，§4.5 或 §4.2 引用。如果空间允许，主文加 1-2 句总结。\n\n**C. 对 K=4 情景预判的更新**\n\nremove_valley 数据几乎确认：如果 K=4 3-seed MMLU 仍然 >42%（情景 B），正确解释是 allocation alignment（per-layer sparsity 均匀性→MMLU 改善），而非 per-layer 训练优势。两个独立实验（K=4 + remove_valley）产生相同 MMLU 水平，这是强证据。\n\n### K=4 训练进度\nwestd-34887：s123 和 s456 都在 step ~1500/2700，~35 min 后训练完成。",
  "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu"
}

[System 触发审查]:
K=4 3-seed downstream 数据落地，需要你做详细论文叙事分析。

## 完整数据

| Task | K=4 3-seed Mean±Std | K=1 3-seed Mean | E2E KL 3-seed Mean | Mismatch解释% | Genuine Coupling% |
|------|---------------------|-----------------|--------------------|----|-----|
| PPL | 8.89±0.01 | 9.05 | 8.81 | 66.7% | 33.3% |
| ARC-c | 40.0±1.3 | 39.9 | 43.7 | 2.6% | **97.4%** |
| WG | 66.9±0.2 | 65.9 | 68.9 | 33.3% | 66.7% |
| MMLU | 42.7±1.4 | 38.6 | 40.7 | >100% | N/A (anomalous) |
| GSM8K | 7.0±1.6 | 5.8 | 7.5 | 70.6% | 29.4% |
| BoolQ | 67.7±7.5 | 64.5 | 73.7 | 34.8% | 65.2% |

## 与 s42 pilot 的关键差异

1. **GSM8K 翻转**: s42 showed -35% (negative), 3-seed shows 70.6% mismatch. s42=5.2% was outlier, s123=7.8/s456=7.9 接近 E2E 7.5.
2. **BoolQ 方差大**: s123=59.1% 严重拉低均值。3-seed mismatch 34.8% vs s42 的 73%.
3. **MMLU 持续反常**: 3-seed 42.7±1.4 均高于 E2E 40.7±1.3。确认为系统性。

## Constraint 实验交叉验证
remove_valley MMLU=44.0 ≈ K=4 MMLU=42.7，两个独立实验确认 allocation alignment 机制。

## 请 Reviewer 分析

1. **修订后的 task 分类**: s42 pilot 用了三类 (mismatch/coupling/anomalous)。3-seed 数据下分类怎么调？ARC-c 97% coupling 是否足以作为"genuine coupling"的 flagship evidence？

2. **"21-48% coupling on downstream"（Abstract/Conclusion 核心 claim）**: K=4 数据是否改变这个数字？K=1 的 21-48% coupling 是 confounded upper bound，K=4 的 genuine coupling 范围是多少？

3. **§4.5 改写建议**: 具体句子级别的改写。注意从 "pilot" 升级为 "3-seed control"。

4. **Abstract/Conclusion 修改**: K=4 从 "pilot suggests" 变成 "3-seed control confirms"？coupling 数字怎么更新？

5. **Appendix Table 14 更新**: 全部更新为 3-seed means±std + 重算 gap closure。

6. **MMLU anomaly 解释**: 用 constraint remove_valley 数据 (44.0) 交叉验证 K=4 (42.7) 的 allocation alignment 机制。这个解释可以多 confident？

155	At 30\%, forward KL (8.81) and JSD (8.90) are near-identical, both outperforming reverse KL (9.26) and dramatically better than MSE (18.52$\pm$1.68; $\sigma$=1.68 vs.\ KL's $\sigma$=0.02, reflecting per-layer signal instability). % exp_id: kl30_ablation_s42, jsd30_ablation_s42, rkl30_ablation_s42, mse30_uniform_s42, mse30_3seed
156	The near-equivalence of KL and JSD suggests the divergence measure matters less than whether the loss optimizes through the output distribution; cross-layer scope provides additional downstream benefits (\S\ref{sec:perlayer}). % exp_id: bce30_uniform_s42, rkl30_downstream_3seed
157	\paragraph{Per-Layer KL: Decomposing Signal Type and Coupling Scope.}
158	\label{sec:perlayer}
159	To isolate cross-layer coupling, we train per-layer KL predictors that mask $K$ random layers per step and compute KL on the full model output, preserving the output-distribution signal without cross-layer gradients.
160	At $K{=}1$, per-layer KL achieves 9.05$\pm$0.02, within 2.7\% of end-to-end KL (8.81$\pm$0.02), while per-layer MSE degrades to 18.52$\pm$1.68 ($2.05\times$ worse). For PPL, signal type\footnote{We use ``signal type'' as shorthand for the training objective's optimization target (output distribution vs.\ intermediate activations); this dimension also entails differences in gradient flow topology and optimization landscape that are not independently controlled.} accounts for up to 97.5\% of the gap (upper bound; signal type and coupling scope are not fully orthogonal).
161	The $K{=}1$ downstream gap conflates true coupling with train--inference mismatch (training masks 1/32 layers; inference applies all 32). A 3-seed $K{=}4$ control\footnote{Actual sparsity 29.6\%; per-layer sparsity std=0.003 (near-uniform).} decomposes this confound (Appendix~\ref{app:k4_downstream}): \emph{coupling-dominated}---ARC-c retains 97\% of the residual gap (40.0$\pm$1.3 vs.\ E2E 43.7$\pm$2.5) and WG retains 67\% (66.9$\pm$0.2 vs.\ 68.9$\pm$0.4), indicating genuine 
…[truncated 2161 chars]

### Tool result

_source line 2152 · 2026-05-22T16:55:23.602791+00:00_

1	Learned sparsity predictors for SwiGLU LLMs predominantly use binary cross-entropy (BCE), training each neuron independently, yet inference allocates budgets globally via top-$k$ ranking.
2	We train lightweight predictors ($<$1\% of base parameters) with forward KL divergence, coupling all mask decisions through the output distribution.
3	On LLaMA-3.1-8B at 30\% sparsity, the KL predictor reduces perplexity by 14.5\% over TEAL at matched sparsity, with commonsense gains but knowledge-intensive decline; post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) becomes redundant.
4	Output-distribution optimization yields masks that diverge 46--68\% from magnitude-based targets; a target-swap ablation suggests these discovered patterns---not the training mechanism itself---as the proximate quality driver.
5	An output-distribution alignment ranking emerges across five loss configurations; a per-layer KL control decomposes this into two dimensions: signal type dominates PPL (up to 97.5\% of the gap at 30\%; upper bound, as dimensions are not fully orthogonal), while coupling scope contributes 10--47\% on downstream tasks (3-seed $K{=}4$-corrected; commonsense 22--47\%, reasoning 10\%); a train--inference mismatch confound in the $K{=}1$ control inflates the $K{=}1$ upper bound (21--48\%).
6	Cross-architecture evaluation on Mistral-7B-v0.3 and Qwen-2.5-14B confirms PPL generalization with scale-dependent downstream effects.
7	This is primarily an empirical study; wall-clock speedup requires sparse kernel support not addressed here.
8

_source line 2154 · 2026-05-22T16:56:07.801313+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Output-distribution alignment is the dominant design dimension for PPL (signal type: up to 97.5\% of the gap at 30\%; upper bound, not fully orthogonal); cross-layer coupling contributes 10--47\% on downstream tasks (3-seed $K{=}4$-corrected; commonsense 22--47\%, GSM8K 10\%); MMLU anomalously favors $K{=}4$, reflecting allocation alignment confirmed by constraint analysis (Appendices~\ref{app:k4_downstream},~\ref{app:constraint}).
5	A predictor ($<$1\% of base) reduces TEAL's PPL by 14.5\% via masks 46--68\% divergent from magnitude; KL outperforms TEAL on commonsense (ARC-c, WG, BoolQ) but underperforms on knowledge tasks (MMLU, GSM8K) across tested scales---a learned allocation trade-off, not an inherent objective limitation (Appendix~\ref{app:constraint}).
6

20	Our contributions are:
21	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
22	\item An output-distribution alignment ranking across five losses, decomposed by a per-layer KL control into signal type (up to 97.5\% of PPL gap; upper bound, not fully orthogonal) and coupling scope (10--47\% on downstream tasks, $K{=}4$-corrected), with task-dependent downstream structure (\S\ref{sec:perlayer});
23	\item Output-distribution losses outperform intermediate losses regardless of coupling scope, with exploratory cross-scale evidence at 7--14B (\S\ref{sec:experiments}).
24	\item A target-swap ablation suggests that KL's discovered mask targets drive inference-stage quality; cross-layer coupling refines downstream fidelity beyond what PPL captures (\S\ref{sec:perlayer}).
25	\item These mask patterns render post-hoc compensation (+67.2M params) redundant at both tested sparsity levels (\S\ref{sec:compensation}).
26	\end{itemize}
27	
28	\begin{figure}[t]
29	\centering
30	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig1_overview.pdf}
31	\caption{Method overview: a lightweight per-layer predictor produces importance scores trained via KL divergence with Gumbel-Sigmoid relaxation. At inference, scores feed TEAL's global top-$k$ allocation without compensation networks.}
32	\label{fig:overview}
33	\end{figure}

301	All compensation networks~\cite{gautam2026fastforward} were trained for 1000 steps (lr=$10^{-4}$, batch size 4, seq length 2048). Training loss plateaus by step 700 across all valid runs: 30\% s42 (final loss 0.70), 30\% s456 (0.68), 50\% s42 (1.07), with fluctuations remaining within 0.08 loss units for the final 300 steps. The compensation network converges but provides no quality improvement over the KL predictor alone, confirming that the redundancy result reflects predictor self-sufficiency rather than insufficient compensation training. % exp_id: kl_comp_30_s42, kl_comp_30_s456, kl_comp_50_s42

_source line 2174 · 2026-05-22T17:33:59.398849+00:00_

[System 触发审查]:
R22 审稿报告到达（3.0 Findings，与 R21 持平）。需要你独立审视我的逐条方案是否合理。

## 完整审稿问题 + 我的初步方案

---

### Must-Fix (Critical)

**N1 [共识] C1 Target-Swap 因果声明过强**
- 问题：Abstract/§4.4 "the discovered patterns—not the training mechanism itself—as the proximate quality driver" 是排除性声明，超出 C1 transferability 证据。s42 用 earlier checkpoint oracle (gap 0.65pp) vs s123/s456 matched retrained (gap ≤0.03pp) 不一致。下游方向不一致（s42 MMLU +1.7pp vs s456 -0.96pp）。
- 方案：**纯写作修复**。将 claim 降级为 transferability：
  - Abstract: "these discovered patterns carry substantial quality information: BCE trained on KL-derived targets approaches KL-level PPL (max Δ≤0.79pp, 3-seed)" → 删除 "not the training mechanism itself"
  - §4.4: 同步降级，加 "while the causal role of coupling during inference remains open"
  - Introduction L11: 调整为 "suggests that mask patterns carry most quality information"
- 这是 R19-N2 的延续（之前已加 "suggests" hedging），现在进一步删除排除性声明。

**N2 [共识] 97.5% Signal Type Decomposition 受混淆**
- 问题：97.5% 在 Abstract/Intro/Conclusion 核心位置，但 K=1 有 train-inference mismatch，K=4 有 allocation alignment artifact (MMLU >100%)，四个 metric 指向不同结论。50% 缺 K=4。
- 方案：
  - **写作修复**：将 "97.5%" 替换为 "the majority of the PPL gap (upper bound; confounded by train-inference mismatch at K=1)"。在 §4.5 正文讨论 orthogonality limitation（从 footnote 提升）。
  - **可选实验**：K=4 at 50% 3-seed。但 R2 rebut 说 50% 是 capacity-limited regime，downstream 接近 random，K=4 在 noise floor 意义不大。考虑到 DDL 3 天 + GPU 空闲，是否值得做？westd-34887 双卡 ~2h 可完成。
- 你觉得 K=4 at 50% 是否值得做？还是写作降级就够？

**N3 [R2 Critical, R1 borderline] MMLU/GSM8K 退化**
- 问题：KL 在所有规模 MMLU/GSM8K 系统性逊于 TEAL。Remove Valley 可恢复 MMLU +3.3pp 但未追踪 task-aware allocation。mode-covering 与 reasoning 退化矛盾未讨论。
- 方案：**纯写作修复**。
  - §4.2: 基于 Remove Valley 结果讨论 task-aware allocation 作为 future direction（"these results suggest task-aware allocation—assigning different per-layer budgets for different task types—as a promising direction"）
  - §4.2 或 §5: 讨论 mode-covering (KL 优化分布宽度) 与 reasoning pathway preservation 的 trade-off
  - 不做新实验（task-aware allocation 是完整研究方向，超出本文 scope）

---

### Should-Fix (Warning)

**W1 [分歧→Warning] 无加速**
- R21 已修复（Abstract empirical study + §1 disclaimer 句子）。R22 仍标 Warning 但已降级。
- 方案：**可在 §1 加一句 speedup 条件**："Realizing wall-clock gains requires sparse GEMM kernels (e.g., Triton block-sparse) that can exploit the predicted masks; we focus on mask quality as the prerequisite step." 如页数允许。

**W2 [分歧→Warning] MaskLLM 区分**
- R21 已修复（§2 三点区分）。R22 Director 判定 Warning。
- 方案：**不再改动**。§2 已有充分区分。Rebuttal 弹药：contextual vs static 是 fundamental difference，不是应用场景差异。

**W3 [共识] Table 2 sparsity mismatch**
- ‡ 脚注 + §4.1 "14.5% at matched 28.5% sparsity" 已有。
- 方案：**写作微调**。§4.1 第一段更显著：把 "14.5% at matched sparsity" 从括号中提出作为主句。如页数允许可在 Table 2 加 TEAL@28.5% 行。

**W4 [共识] KL global 50% 下游缺 3-seed**
- Table 2 L61 PPL 有 ±0.15 (3-seed) 但 ARC-c/WG/GSM8K 无 ±。
- 方案：**需要确认数据**。检查 registry 中 kl50_global_s123/s456 是否有 downstream eval。如果有数据直接补表；如果没有，让 Agent 在 westd-34887 补跑 eval（eval only，~30min/seed）。
- 你帮忙确认一下 registry 中这两个 exp_id 是否有 ARC-c/WG/MMLU/GSM8K 数据？

**W5 [共识] 规模泛化仅 7-14B**
- 方案：**纯写作**。Limitations 加 "evaluation is limited to 7–14B; whether the PPL-downstream trade-off persists at ≥30B scales remains open"。

**W6 [共识] Figure 2 global/uniform**
- 之前 Paper Agent 尝试加 caption 注释但因页数溢出移除。
- 方案：**压缩其他内容后再尝试**。如 Table 2 caption 精简腾出空间。

**W7 [共识] Abstract 第5句密度过高**
- 方案：**写作修复**。将 ~60 词嵌套句拆成两句：一句说 signal type dominates PPL，一句说 coupling contributes downstream（配合 N2 的 97.5% 降级）。

**W8 [共识] Conclusion 引用 BoolQ**
- BoolQ variance 极高 (std=11.5)，仅在 Appendix K=4 表。
- 方案：**写作修复**。删除 BoolQ 引用，改引 HellaSwag/PIQA（Table 11, stable）。Conclusion 加一句 limitations 提及。

---

### Minor

**M1**: Table 2 注释 — 已精简（R21 patch），保持现状
**M2**: §3.5 ε=0.02 — 加一句 "chosen to accommodate ±2% sparsity estimation noise from Gumbel sampling"
**M3**: BCE wall-clock — 加解释："BCE requires only predictor forward; KL requires both predictor and dense forward pass, but KL converges in 1/2.7× steps"
**M4**: "Adaptive Penalty Controller" — 改为 "Geometric Penalty Schedule" 或 "Penalty Doubling Schedule"
**M5**: FastForward reference — 让 Agent 检查是否已被会议接收
**M6**: Limitations 加 sequence length — 一句话
**M7**: SPON 分类 — §3.7 加一句解释

---

### Questions → Rebuttal 弹药

**Q1 Reverse C1**: "An interesting control we leave for future work. Current C1 establishes mask transferability; reverse C1 would test coupling's value on suboptimal targets—complementary but distinct."
**Q2 50% K=4**: 见 N2 讨论。如做实验则回答数据；如不做，解释 capacity-limited regime + downstream noise floor。
**Q3 task-aware allocation**: 在 §4.2 讨论为 future direction (见 N3 方案)。
**Q4 14.5% 呈现**: §4.1 已标注 "at matched sparsity"，可进一步加显著性。
**Q5 §3.6 vs C1 关系**: 写作澄清——"coupling discovers better masks during training; C1 shows these masks transfer across training mechanisms, suggesting coupling's primary value is in the search phase rather than inference-time maintenance"
**Q6 λ_max**: Appendix 简要讨论 "increasing λ_max beyond 5000 caused training instability (loss spikes); the 1.5% sparsity gap is within acceptable tolerance"

---

### Missing References

**MR1 LaRoSA**: 需 WebSearch 确认。如存在且高度相关，在 §2 引用。
**MR2 Geva et al. 2023**: 已在 bib 中。在 §4.2 MMLU regression 讨论中引用。

---

### 优先级排序

**Tier 1（必做，直接影响分数）**: N1 写作降级、N2 写作降级 + 97.5%→定性、N3 §4.2 深化分析、W7 Abstract 简化、W8 Conclusion BoolQ 修复
**Tier 2（应做，提升严谨性）**: W3 sparsity mismatch 显著化、W4 50% 3-seed 补数据、W1 speedup 条件、M2-M4 写作微调
**Tier 3（可选）**: K=4 at 50% 实验（N2）、W6 Figure 2 注释、MR1/MR2 引用

请审查：
1. 我的方案是否遗漏了关键点？
2. N2 的 K=4 at 50% 实验值不值得做？
3. N1 的降级措辞是否足够？
4. W4 的 kl50_global_s123/s456 下游数据请帮忙确认 registry。
5. 整体策略是否能有效从 3.0 提到 3.5？

{
  "text": "## R17 — 审稿方案独立审查\n\n### 1. 遗漏的关键点\n\n**[severity: warning] Table 2 KL global 50% MMLU 数据不一致**\nbench_kl50_global (s42) 在 registry 中 MMLU=0.2789 (27.89%)，但 Table 2 L61 显示 28.1。差异 0.21pp。请核实数据来源——如果表格用的是两 seed 平均（s42=27.89 + s456=28.0 → 均值 27.95 ≈ 28.0，仍不是 28.1），或者有其他来源。\n\n**[severity: info] perlayer_kl_50_s42 registry 状态仍为 ANALYZING**\n已完成（PPL=34.85），但 registry 未更新为 DONE。次要问题但影响自动化监控。\n\n**[severity: info] GSM8K K=4 per-seed gap closure 符号翻转**\n单独说一下你可能已经注意到的：K=4 GSM8K gap closure 按单 seed 算是 s42=-38%, s123=+118%, s456=+122%——方向完全相反。3-seed 均值 70.6% 掩盖了极端不稳定性。论文当前处理（±1.6 std）是合理的，但审稿人如果自己算 per-seed 值会发现这一点。建议在 §4.5 或 Appendix K=4 表 footnote 加一句\"individual-seed gap closure varies widely due to GSM8K's high sampling noise at 1319 examples\"。\n\n### 2. N2: K=4 at 50% 值不值得做？\n\n**建议做，但限定 scope：单 seed (s42)，PPL-only，不跑 downstream。**\n\n理由：\n- 已有 perlayer_kl_50_s42 (K=1): PPL=34.85。E2E KL 50%: PPL=11.84。缺口巨大 (+194%)。\n- K=4 at 50% PPL 的值无论落在哪里都有信息量：\n  - 如果 ~15-20：mismatch 贡献大，K=4 能关闭 ~60-80% gap → 加强\"高 sparsity 下 mismatch 更严重\"叙事\n  - 如果 ~30+：coupling 在 50% 依然主导 → 加强\"高 sparsity 下 coupling 更重要\"叙事\n- downstream 在 50% 接近 random noise floor（MMLU ~28%, GSM8K ~1.3%），3-seed downstream 无意义\n- GPU 成本：训练 ~83 min + PPL eval ~5 min，westd-34887 一块卡足够\n\n**不做的论据**（你提到的 R2 noise floor）只适用于 downstream，不适用于 PPL。PPL 在 50% 仍然有信噪比（11.84 vs 34.85 的缺口巨大）。做了可以在 rebuttal 直接给数据回答 Q2，比写理由强得多。\n\n### 3. N1 降级措辞是否足够？\n\n**方向正确，但需注意一个额外问题。**\n\n你的方案将 \"not the training mechanism itself\" 替换为 transferability 语言——这是对的。但审稿人指出的 **s42 oracle checkpoint vs s123/s456 matched retrained 不一致** 也需要在论文中交代。建议：\n\n- §4.4 target-swap 部分加一句实验细节说明：\"For s42, we use an earlier checkpoint (2000 steps) as the oracle source, while s123/s456 use seed-matched fully converged checkpoints（C1 ablation 的 setup difference）.\"\n- 或在 Appendix 加完整的 C1 实验设计表（每个 seed 的 oracle source + gap）\n\n如果不说明，审稿人在 rebuttal 阶段会追问 s42 gap (0.65pp) 为什么比 s123/s456 (≤0.03pp) 大 20 倍。\n\n**降级措辞建议修改一处**：你提议 \"these discovered patterns carry substantial quality information\"——\"substantial\" 仍然偏强。s42 0.65pp gap 说明不是完美 transfer。建议 \"carry most of the quality information (max Δ≤0.79pp, 3-seed)\"——和 data 绑定更紧。\n\n### 4. W4: kl50_global 3-seed downstream 数据\n\nRegistry 查证结果：\n- **s42 (bench_kl50_global)**: ✅ 完整 — ARC-c=32.85, WG=63.46, MMLU=27.89, GSM8K=1.29\n- **s456 (kl50_global_s456_eval)**: ✅ 完整 — ARC-c=36.9, WG=64.0, MMLU=28.0, GSM8K=3.5\n- **s123 (kl50_global_s123_reeval)**: ❌ FAILED (EXIT_CODE=1)\n\n**缺 s123 的 50% global downstream**。re-run 约 30 min 单卡。\n\n但考虑到 50% downstream 接近 noise floor（上述 ARC-c s42=32.85 vs s456=36.9，差 4pp，noise 很大），3-seed std 的信息量有限。**做了是好的，但优先级低于 N2 的 K=4 50% PPL 实验。**\n\n同时注意 **KL 50% uniform 已有 3-seed ± 数据**（Table 2 L62），只有 global 行缺。\n\n### 5. 整体策略能否从 3.0 → 3.5？\n\n**判断：有合理概率，但不保证。**\n\n向好因素：\n- N1 降级是诚实的 retreat，审稿人通常正面评价 honest acknowledgement\n- N2 从定量降为定性 + 新数据（K=4 50% PPL）直接回答疑问\n- N3 有 remove_valley 因果证据支撑，不是空谈\n- 写作修复覆盖面广（W1-W8 全覆盖）\n\n风险因素：\n- 3.0 → 3.5 需要审稿人改变评分。R22 的 3.0 表明他对 novelty 有根本性疑虑，单靠写作修复可能不够\n- MMLU/GSM8K 退化仍是系统性弱点，\"future direction\" 的回答可能被视为 dodge\n- 缺乏加速结果（W1）是 applied ML 审稿人的硬伤——即使标了 empirical study\n\n**建议增加一个策略支点**：rebuttal 中用一段专门强调 **remove_valley 实验的因果洞察**——这是 R22 可能没有完全理解的新贡献。它证明了 \"learned allocation 不是随机的，middle-layer protection 有明确的 task-dependent trade-off\"。这比 \"future direction\" 更有说服力，因为它展示了可操作的因果理解。\n\n**Tier 1 中建议加上 N3 的 remove_valley 数字化讨论**（不只是 conceptual future direction，而是 \"remove_valley: MMLU +3.3pp but ARC-c -4.4pp, confirming the allocation is a trade-off, not a failure\"）。这直接回击 R2 的 \"KL 系统性逊于 TEAL\" 论点。",
  "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu"
}

  "text": "## R18 — W4 + N2 实验结果审查\n\n### W4: kl50_global_s123 downstream 数据落地\n\n**[severity: warning] 3-seed 统计量与 Table 2 当前单 seed 值有显著差异**\n\nkl50_global_s123_downstream_v2 完成，JSON 已验证。3-seed 汇总：\n\n| Task | s42 | s123 (新) | s456 | 3-seed mean±std |\n|------|-----|------|------|-----------------|\n| ARC-c | 32.85 | 39.16 | 36.90 | **36.3±3.2** |\n| WG | 63.46 | 64.01 | 64.00 | **63.8±0.3** |\n| MMLU | 27.89 | 29.08 | 28.00 | **28.3±0.7** |\n| GSM8K | 1.29 | 3.41 | 3.50 | **2.7±1.3** |\n\n**Table 2 L61 当前显示 s42 单 seed 值（32.85, 63.46, 28.1, 1.29），s42 恰好是 ARC-c 和 GSM8K 的最差 seed。** 更新为 3-seed mean 后：\n- ARC-c: 32.85 → 36.3 (+3.45pp) — KL global 表现比表中呈现的好得多\n- GSM8K: 1.29 → 2.7 (+1.4pp)\n- MMLU: 28.1 → 28.3 (微调)\n\n**副作用**：更新后 KL global ARC-c (36.3±3.2) 超过 KL uniform (33.8±1.2)，论文叙事中 \"global vs uniform trade-off\" 需要相应调整。但 ARC-c 的 std=3.2 与 uniform 的 std=1.2 重叠，差异不显著。\n\n此外，Table 2 当前 MMLU=28.1 与 bench_kl50_global registry 值 27.89 不符（已在 R17 标记），3-seed 修正为 28.3±0.7 顺便解决此问题。\n\n### N2: K=4 50% 训练完成，PPL eval 待补\n\nperlayer_kl_k4_50_s42 训练成功完成（1000 steps, 31 min）：\n- avg_kl = 0.1267（vs K=1 50%: 0.0282 — K=4 训练 KL 更高，符合预期，因为 K=4 扰动更大）\n- avg_sparsity = 0.496 ✅\n- Checkpoint 已落盘：predictor_perlayer_kl_k4.pt (152 MB)\n\n**[severity: warning] PPL eval 中断**——日志最后一行 \"Eval mode: all layers masked simultaneously\" 后静默退出。Checkpoint 完好，仅需 re-run PPL eval（~5 min）。\n\n这个数据对 N2 rebuttal 至关重要：\n- K=1 50% PPL: 34.85\n- E2E KL 50% PPL: 11.84（±0.15）\n- K=4 50% PPL: **待补**\n- 无论 K=4 落在哪里，都能量化 50% 下 mismatch vs coupling 的分解\n\n**建议**：Agent 在 westd-34887 补跑 `benchmark_eval.py --tasks wikitext2` 指向 K=4 50% checkpoint，单卡 5 分钟即可。",
